2. **API Key Injection:** When I started the process on the virtual device, the application successfully and perfectly copied my API key from Google AI Studio, but it didn't place it where it belonged. 
   - Currently, users must open the documentation page to input their API key. 
   - If that process is too long for automatic injection, relocate the input field to a place that is easy for the application to inject the retrieved API key into automatically.
   - The goal is for the retrieved/copied API key to automatically go to the correct location in the application so that the app functions seamlessly using it without manual user input. (Android displaying that the API key has been copied is fine).

3. **Preserve Text Injection:** Do not break the current state of the application's text injection (such as when using a floating bubble in Google Keep or other notes). Do not tamper with the text injection or cause issues like "empty note discarded" notices. 

In summary: Make the application non-functional without an API key, and ensure the automatically copied API key is successfully injected into the correct place so the application works with it, while leaving the general text injection behavior completely untouched.
